Full local L-BFGS penalty implementation details:
```
def smooth_max_and_grad(h, beta, target):
    n=len(h); counts=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); f=counts*(2.0/n)
    fm=f.max(); exp=np.exp(beta*(f-fm)); s=exp.sum(); w=exp/s
    obj = fm + np.log(s)/beta + pen*(sum-target)^2? If using logsum directly O = (fm + log(s)/beta). gradient w * grad_f * dx? Since f=counts*dx, grad_f = grad_counts*dx.
```
But softmax over all f; if beta high, w concentrates max. Use logsumstable. The maximum term included.
Gradient of f: Need compute counts gradients. Let's implement `counts_gradient(h, w)`:
The f vector from np.correlate(h,1-h) maybe orientation c_i = ∑ h[i+k]*(1-h[i]). We'll derive gradient vector g (length n) such that for counts (not dx), d c_j = dot(g, dh).
Loop over lag index idx=0..2n-2, k=idx-(n-1). If w_idx ==0 skip. i range: i from max(0,-k) to min(n, n-k). Let lower = max(0,-k); upper = min(n, n-k). For each i in range:
 term h[i+k]*(1-h[i])
 derivative wrt h[i+k]: (1-h[i])
 derivative wrt h[i]: -h[i+k]
So:
 g[lower+k:upper+k] += w * (1 - h[lower:upper])
 g[lower:upper] -= w * h[lower+k:upper+k]
This works for any k signed. For k=0: term h(i)(1-h(i)) derivative (1-2h). Our code: first adds (1-h) at same, second subtracts h, total 1-2h. Correct.
This loop for each gradient. For n=400, 799 loops. `if w_idx > 1e-12` to skip. In L-BFGS, beta moderate, many w small; but early beta small ~all active. Could optimize for speed: if beta small, all weights >0; loop 800 slices; each slice operations. L-BFGS 100 iterations =>80k loops, possibly okay but maybe slow in Python. Need optimize.
We can compute gradient via convolutions for count matrix more efficiently:
For signed k, first term at index j: sum over i=j-k h? Let's use np.convolve/correlate.
Define w array length 2n-1 indexed by lag k: w[k+n-1]. Let a=1-h, b=h.
First term g1[j] = ∑_{k} w[k] a[j-k]? where pair h[j] first, h[j-k] second. Conditions j∈[0,n), i=j-k ∈[0,n). k can signed. Let w_s indexed 0..2n-2 for k=-(n-1)..n-1. ```
OBJ_BETA=1.0; OBJ_LAM=1e3; OBJ_TARGET=200.0; OBJ_RIDGE=1e-8
def _objectives(x):
    h=np.asarray(x)
    counts=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    f=counts*(2.0/x.size)
    m=f.max(); e=np.exp(OBJ_BETA*(f-m)); s=e.sum()
    logsum=m+np.log(s)
    sumerr=h.sum()-OBJ_TARGET
    return logsum/OBJ_BETA + OBJ_LAM*sumerr*sumerr + OBJ_RIDGE*np.dot(h,h)
```

```

def counts_grad_w(h, wfull):
    n=len(h)
    A=1.0-h
    g1=np.convolve(wfull, A, 'full')
    g2=np.convolve(wfull[::-1], h, 'full')
    return g1[n-1:2*n-1] - g2[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Project feasible: to ensure sum exactly n/2. Use iterative water-filling; or simple clip then scale but scaling may clip. For 400, do loop 100:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1-h; s=room.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += diff/s*room
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += diff/s*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final exact adjust by distributing over interior? If sum not exactly due to rounding, do:
    # find indices with 0<h<1, adjust first? But floating exact not required? They say sum==n/2 exactly maybe evaluation uses sum; ensure close 1e-12.
    return h
```
To get exact sum after final clip, we can adjust:
```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
 target=n/2; diff=target-h.sum(); if abs(diff)<1e-13 return; if diff>0 add to entries <1 proportional room else subtract from entries >0; then clip; maybe second pass.
```
The project loop does enough.

Global L-BFGS:
```
OBJ_BETA=1.0; OBJ_LAM=1e4; OBJ_TARGET=200.0; OBJ_RIDGE=1e-8
def obj_values(h): ... return obj, f, m, e
def obj_global(x): ... return obj
def jac_global(x): ... return grad
def lbfgs_refine(start,beta,lam,maxiter=200):
   global OBJ_BETA, OBJ_LAM, OBJ_TARGET, OBJ_RIDGE
   OBJ_BETA=beta; OBJ_LAM=lam; OBJ_TARGET=start.size/2; OBJ_RIDGE=1e-8
   res=opt.minimize(obj_global, start, jac=jac_global, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=[(0,1)]*start.size, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'maxfun':1000})
   return project_feasible(res.x, len(start))
```

Binary local search function:
```
def binary_threshold(h):
   n=len(h); idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]; b=np.zeros(n); b[idx[:n//2]]=1.0; return b

Local search:
```
def binary_local(h0, fit_time):
   n=len(h0); h=np.clip(np.round(h0),0,1); adjust to n//2 by threshold if needed: sort.
   best=eval(h)
   rng=np.random.RandomState(123)
   temp=best
   while time.time()<stop:
      # random block of 10 flips? single
      i=rng.randint(n)
      h2=h.copy(); h2[i]^=1
      c2=eval(h2)
      if c2<best-1e-15: best=c2; h=h2; continue
      if rng.random()<math.exp((best-c2)/temp): h=h2
      temp=max(0.01, temp*0.99)
   return h,best
```

Now binary local search function more detailed.
```
def make_binary(h):
    n=len(h); b=np.zeros(n); idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]; b[idx[:n//2]]=1.0; return b
def binary_local_search(b, stop_time, rng):
    n=len(b); best=eval_c5(b)
    h=b.copy()
    temp=0.05
    no=0
    while time.time()<stop_time:
        i=rng.randint(n)
        h2=h.copy(); h2[i]^=1; c2=eval_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best - 1e-15:
            h=h2; best=c2; no=0
            if best * 1 < target? no need.
        elif rng.random() < math.exp((best-c2)/temp):
            h=h2; no+=1
        else:
            no+=1
        temp=max(0.005, temp*0.999)
    return h,best
```

  h=project_feasible

    if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: break
    if diff>0:

    else: idx=np.where(h>0.0)
    if len==0 break
    # distribute equally to 1 interior or bounded

       ...

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+max(60,budget_s)-5
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  try:
     h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
  except Exception:
     h=triangle_h(400)
  n=len(h)
  if n<64: n=400; h=triangle_h(400)
  h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h)
  # candidates
  for cand in [triangle_h(n), ...]: update
  # parent refine
  t_end=t0+0.35*budget
  best_h,best_c=full_refine(best_h,t_end,rng)
  # PL search
  t_end=t0+0.65*budget
  if time<t_end: best_h,best_c=pl_search(best_h,t_end,seed,rng)
  # full refine PL/best
  t_end=t0+0.85*budget
  best_h,best_c=full_refine(best_h,t_end,rng)
  # binary optional
  t_end=t0+0.93*budget
  if time<t_end and best_c>0.3807: maybe always
     b=make_binary(best_h); best_h,best_c=binary_local(b,t_end,rng,best_h,best_c)
  # final n-sweep
  t_end=deadline-2
  best_h,best_c=final_n_sweep(best_h,best_c,t_end)
  # if best_h n different, quick refine maybe
  if time<deadline-10: best_h,best_c=full_refine(best_h, deadline-2, rng, one_beta?)
  return project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h)), eval_c5(...), len
```

def pl_search(hbest, stop, seed, rng):
   global KNOT_R, GRID_R, GRID_N
   n=len(hbest)
   m=13
   KNOT_R=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   GRID_R=np.abs((np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n - 1.0)
   GRID_N=n
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*m
   best_c=eval...
   vals0=profile_vals(hbest,n,m)
   # DE if time allow: require at least 60s. Use maxiter maybe 80.
   try:
      res=differential_evolution(pl_objective, bounds, seed=seed+1, maxiter=60, popsize=12, tol=1e-8, polish=False)
      if res.fun < best_c: update from res.x
   except: pass
   # Nelder from vals0 and res.x
   for x0 in [vals0, res.x if ...]:
      if time>stop-30: break
      try:
        res2=minimize(pl_objective,x0,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':300,'fatol':1e-10})
        if res2.fun < best_c: update
      except: pass
   return best_h,best_c
```

Need `profile_vals` if `m` parameter. Use KNOT_R.
```
def profile_vals(h,n,m):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n; r=np.abs(x-1.0); order=np.argsort(r); rs=r[order]; hs=h[order]
   uniq, inv, counts = np.unique(rs, return_inverse=True, return_counts=True)
   sums=np.bincount(inv, weights=hs)
   avg=sums/counts
   return np.interp(np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m), uniq, avg)
```
Since rs min>0, np.interp at 0 extrapolates to first avg (near center). Good.

`interpolate_profile(vals,n)`: `x=(arange(n)+0.5)*2/n; r=...; h=np.interp(r, np.linspace(0,1,len(vals)), vals); project`. We can use in PL objective to avoid global GRID_R. But computing r each eval overhead. Precompute global.

def full_refine(h, stop, betas):
  best=project; bestc=eval
  for beta in betas:
    if time.time() > stop - 40: break
    h2=lbfgs_refine(best, beta, 5e4, 300)

  # perturbation restarts if stop-time > 100
  if rng is not None:

       if time.time() > stop-80: break

       start=project(best + scale*noise,n)
       for b in [400,1600]: ...
  return best,bestc

Implementation:
```
def binary_swaps(b, stop, rng, cur_h, cur_c):
  n=len(b); h=b.copy(); curc=eval,h best...
  temp=0.01
  while time<stop:
    ones=np.nonzero(h)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(1-h)[0]
    if len(ones)==0 or zeros==0: continue
    i=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; j=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
    h2=h.copy(); h2[i],h2[j]=0,1
    c2=eval(h2)
    if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
       h=h2; curc=c2; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c2
    else:
       if c2 <= curc + 0.01 and rng.random() < math.exp((curc-c2)/temp): h=h2; curc=c2
       temp=max(0.002,temp*0.999)
  return best_h,best_c
```

Parameter PL objective uses `eval_c5(project_feasible(h,n))`. `project_feasible` could be a bit slow in DE (13k times). But n=400, loop 200 maybe heavy. We can optimize project for PL by using simple scaling clipping? Let's make project efficient:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
  target=n/2.0
  for _ in range(50):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    if diff>0:
       room=1-h; s=room.sum()
       if s<=1e-14: break
       h += diff/s*room
    else:
       s=h.sum()
       if s<=1e-14: break
       h += diff/s*h
  return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
50 loops. Each loop sum and vector operations. DE 13k*50*400 ~260M operations, maybe 1s? could be 5s. We can reduce loops to 20 enough. Sum error converges linearly. Use 20. Also if diff small initial, break. For target exactly sum after project, 20 enough. Let's use 30. Need feasible. If area very off due to values out of bounds, 30 okay. Could use more robust water-fill:
- To add diff>0: if h.some<1, distribute proportionally to room; if after distribution still diff>1e-12 because all room sum too small? It will converge geometrically. 30 fine.
Use `if abs(diff) < 1e-13`.

Pseudo code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

OBJ_BETA=1.0
OBJ_LAM=1e4
OBJ_TARGET=1.0
OBJ_RIDGE=1e-8

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if h.size != n: h=np.resize(h,n) # maybe not
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(30):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; s=room.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += (diff/s)*room
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += (diff/s)*h
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # exact adjust
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
        if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1.0)[0]
        else: idx=np.where(h>0.0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            # add evenly with clipping
            for i in idx:
                if diff==0.0: break
                if diff>0:
                    add=min(1.0-h[i], diff)
                else:
                    add=max(-h[i], diff)
                h[i]+=add
                diff-=add
    return h
```
Fine. Note if `np.resize` not desired; h length should n. If not, just return. Keep simple.

`eval_c5`: if `h.size==0` return 1.

`counts_grad_w`:
```
def counts_grad_w(h,w):
   n=h.size
   if n==1: return np.array([ (1-2*h[0]) * w[0] ])
   g=np.convolve(w,1.0-h,'full') - np.convolve(w[::-1],h,'full')
   return g[n-1:2*n-1]
```
But `np.convolve` returns ndarray; if w sum large? okay. Need ensure `2*n-1` maybe indexing length: full length len(w)+n-1 =3n-2. Slice start n-1 length n? `n-1:2*n-1` gives n elements (end exclusive 2n-1). For n=3, slice [2:5] length3. Good.
If n=1 length w 1, convolve length2, slice 0:2 length2? But n-1=0,2*n-1=1 length1. okay.

`_smooth_core`:
```
def _smooth_core(h):
   n=h.size
   counts=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
   f=counts*(2.0/n)
   m=f.max()
   e=np.exp(OBJ_BETA*(f-m))
   s=e.sum()
   return f,m,e,s
```
If `s=0`? no.

`_obj`, `_jac` as above.

`lbfgs_refine`:
```
def lbfgs_refine(start, beta, lam, maxiter=150):
    global OBJ_BETA, OBJ_LAM, OBJ_TARGET, OBJ_RIDGE
    start=project_feasible(start, start.size)
    OBJ_BETA=float(beta); OBJ_LAM=float(lam); OBJ_TARGET=0.5*start.size; OBJ_RIDGE=1e-8
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*start.size
    try:
       res=minimize(_obj, start, jac=_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxiter*8,'ftol':1e-18})
       h2=project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x,float), start.size)
       return h2
    except Exception:
       return start
```
If `res.status` not success but res.x still.

`full_refine` with rng optional:
```
def full_refine(h, stop, betas, rng=None):
   best=project_feasible(h, h.size); bestc=eval_c5(best)
   n=best.size
   for beta in betas:
      if time.time() > stop - 35: break
      h2=lbfgs_refine(best,beta,1e5,160)
      c2=eval_c5(h2)
      if c2 < bestc - 1e-14:
         best=h2; bestc=c2
   if rng is not None and stop - time.time() > 90:
      for scale in (0.05,0.02):
         if time.time() > stop-60: break
         noise=rng.randn(n)
         kernel=np.ones(7)/7.0
         noise=np.convolve(noise,kernel,'same')
         start=project_feasible(best + scale*noise, n)
         for beta in (400,1600):
            if time.time()>stop-20: break
            h2=lbfgs_refine(start,beta,1e5,100)
            c2=eval_c5(h2)
            if c2<bestc-1e-14: best=h2; bestc=c2
   return best,bestc
```
Maybe noise scale 0.05 keeps near; if parent near optimum, could improve. Use `np.convolve` creates same length? `mode='same'` length n. Good.

`try_candidate`:
```
def try_candidate(h, cur_h, cur_c):
   if h is None: return cur_h,cur_c
   n=abs? if len(h)==0 return
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   c=eval_c5(h)
   if c < cur_c: return h,c
   return cur_h,cur_c
```
Maybe if c equal no.

`grid_r(n)`:
```
def grid_r(n): return np.abs((np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)-1.0)
```
`triangle_h(n)`: `h=grid_r(n); return project_feasible(h,n)`.
`sin_h(n)`: x=...; h=0.5*(1-np.cos(np.pi*(x-1))); project.
`power_h(n,p)`: h=grid_r(n)**p * (p+1)/2; project. If p=0, r^0=1 center? ignore.

PL globals:
```
PL_GRID_R=None; PL_KNOTS=None; PL_N=400
def pl_objective(vals):
    h=np.interp(PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, vals)
    h=project_feasible(h, PL_N)
    return eval_c5(h)
```
`profile_vals` as above.

`pl_search`:
```
def pl_search(hbest, stop, seed, rng):
    global PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, PL_N
    n=hbest.size; m=13
    PL_GRID_R=grid_r(n); PL_KNOTS=np.linspace(0,1,m); PL_N=n
    bounds=[(-0.5,1.5)]*m
    best=hbest; bestc=eval_c5(best)
    vals0=profile_vals(best,n,m)
    starts=[vals0, np.linspace(0,1,m), profile_vals(sin_h(n),n,m), profile_vals(power_h(n,1.5),n,m)]
    # dedup maybe not
    # DE if enough time
    if stop - time.time() > 80:
        try:
           res=differential_evolution(pl_objective,bounds,seed=seed+1,maxiter=35,popsize=12,tol=1e-8,polish=False)
           if res.fun < bestc:
               vh=res.x; h=project_feasible(np.interp(PL_GRID_R,PL_KNOTS,vh),n); best,h... if better
           starts.append(vh)
        except Exception: pass
    # Nelder from starts limited
    for x0 in starts:
        if time.time()>stop-25: break
        x0=np.clip(np.asarray(x0,float), -1, 2) # maybe no clip? project; but Nelder might go wild; clip each? objective handles.
        try:
           res=minimize(pl_objective,x0,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':250,'fatol':1e-10,'xatol':1e-8})
           vh=res.x; c=pl_objective(vh)
           if c < bestc:
              h=project_feasible(np.interp(PL_GRID_R,PL_KNOTS,vh),n); if eval<h update; maybe c from eval not pl? same.
        except: pass
    return best,bestc
```
DE `bounds=[(-0.5,1.5)]*m`. If res.fun maybe not exact due to project, but okay.

h=project_feasible(np.interp(PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, vh), PL_N); c=eval_c5(h); if c<bestc: best=h.copy(); bestc=c
```

`binary_local`:
```
def binary_local(b, stop, rng, cur_h, cur_c):
   n=b.size
   if n%2: return cur_h,cur_c
   b=np.zeros(n); b[:n//2]=1? no, use make.
   cur=make_binary(b)
   curc=eval_c5(cur)
   best_h=cur_h; best_c=cur_c
   if curc < best_c:
      best_h=cur.copy(); best_c=curc
   h=cur.copy(); temp=0.01
   while time.time()<stop:
       ones=np.nonzero(h)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(1-h)[0]
       if ones.size==0 or zeros.size==0: break
       i=int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)]); j=int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
       h2=h.copy(); h2[i]=0.0; h2[j]=1.0
       c2=eval_c5(h2)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
           h=h2; curc=c2; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c2
       elif c2 <= curc + 0.01 and rng.random() < math.exp((curc-c2)/temp):
           h=h2; curc=c2
       temp=max(0.002, temp*0.999)
   return best_h,best_c
```
This may spend 60s many evals; okay. If no binary better, continuous remains.

`final_n_sweep`:
```
def resample_eval(h,n1):
   n0=h.size
   x0=(np.arange(n0)+0.5)*2.0/n0
   x1=(np.arange(n1)+0.5)*2.0/n1
   h1=np.interp(x1,x0,h).astype(float)
   h1=project_feasible(h1,n1)
   return h1, eval_c5(h1)

def final_n_sweep(h, cur_c, stop):
   best_h=h; best_c=cur_c
   for n1 in range(64,1025,32):
       if time.time()>stop: break
       h1,c1=resample_eval(best_h,n1)
       if c1<best_c:
           best_h=h1; best_c=c1
   return best_h,best_c
```

`make_binary` with even:
```
def make_binary(h):
   n=h.size
   if n % 2 != 0:
      # return continuous? We return None
      return None
   b=np.zeros(n)
   idx=np.argpartition(h, n//2)[-n//2:] if n//2>0 else []
   b[idx]=1.0
   return b
```

Let's write compact code:

```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, float), 0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(25):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13: break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h; s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-14: break
            h += (d / s) * r
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-14: break
            h += (d / s) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = t - h.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-12:
        idx = np.where(h < 1.0 if d > 0 else h > 0.0)[0]
        for i in idx:
            if d == 0.0: break
            add = min(1.0 - h[i], d) if d > 0 else max(-h[i], d)
            h[i] += add; d -= add
    return h
```
Good.

`eval_c5`:
```
def eval_c5(h):
    n = h.size
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, 'full')) * (2.0 / n))
```

`counts_grad_w`:
```
def counts_grad_w(h, w):
    n = h.size
    a = np.convolve(w, 1.0 - h, 'full')
    b = np.convolve(w[::-1], h, 'full')
    return a[n-1:2*n-1] - b[n-1:2*n-1]
```

`_smooth_core` etc.

`full_refine` compress:
```
def full_refine(h, stop, betas, rng=None):
    best=project_feasible(h,h.size); bestc=eval_c5(best); n=best.size
    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() > stop-30: break
        h2=lbfgs_refine(best,beta,1e5,150); c2=eval_c5(h2)
        if c2 < bestc: best,bestc=h2,c2
    if rng is not None and time.time() < stop-70:
        noise=rng.randn(n); k=np.ones(7)/7.0; noise=np.convolve(noise,k,'same')
        for sc in (0.04,0.015):
            if time.time()>stop-20: break
            st=project_feasible(best+sc*noise,n)
            h2=lbfgs_refine(st,800,1e5,100); c2=eval_c5(h2)
            if c2<bestc: best,bestc=h2,c2
    return best,bestc
```
Only one noise direction; maybe random multiple not. Could generate inside loop with different rng but okay.

PL search compress. Use global variables. `starts` list.
```
def pl_search(hbest, stop, seed, rng):
   global PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, PL_N
   n=hbest.size; m=15
   PL_GRID_R=grid_r(n); PL_KNOTS=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); PL_N=n
   best=project_feasible(hbest,n); bestc=eval_c5(best)
   bounds=[(-0.5,1.5)]*m
   v0=profile_vals(best,n,m)
   starts=[v0, np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m), profile_vals(sin_h(n),n,m), profile_vals(power_h(n,1.5),n,m)]
   if stop-time.time()>60:
      try:
         res=differential_evolution(pl_objective,bounds,seed=seed,maxiter=30,popsize=12,tol=1e-8,polish=False)
         if res.fun < bestc:
            h=project_feasible(np.interp(PL_GRID_R,PL_KNOTS,res.x),n); c=eval_c5(h)
            if c<bestc: best,bestc=h,c
         starts.append(res.x)
      except Exception: pass
   for x0 in starts:
      if time.time()>stop-20: break
      try:
         res=minimize(pl_objective,np.asarray(x0,float).copy(),method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':200,'fatol':1e-10})
         h=project_feasible(np.interp(PL_GRID_R,PL_KNOTS,res.x),n); c=eval_c5(h)
         if c<bestc: best,bestc=h,c
      except Exception: pass
   return best,bestc
```
DE maxiter=30, pop=180 ->5400 evals. Good. If time 60s, okay.
Potential: If DE res.fun uses pl_objective but res.x maybe; if res.fun > bestc, still add res.x? Could be useful; but to avoid extra, only if not. Add unconditionally after DE? We do. If DE worst, Nelder may waste. Use `if res.fun < bestc + 0.005:` maybe.

Nelder from `np.linspace` and power; good.

```
def make_binary(h):
   n=h.size
   if n%2: return None
   k=n//2; b=np.zeros(n)
   if k: b[np.argpartition(h,n-k)[-k:]] = 1.0
   return b
```

   best=h; bestc=c
   for n1 in range(32,1025,16):

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize
try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None

OBJ_BETA = 16.0
OBJ_LAM = 1e5
OBJ_TARGET = 1.0
OBJ_RIDGE = 1e-10

PL_GRID_R = None
PL_KNOTS = None
PL_N = 400


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(25):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        idx = np.where(h < 1.0 if diff > 0.0 else h > 0.0)[0]
        for i in idx:
            if diff == 0.0:
                break
            if diff > 0.0:
                add = min(1.0 - h[i], diff)
            else:
                add = max(-h[i], diff)
            h[i] += add
            diff -= add
    return h


def eval_c5(h):
    n = h.size
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, "full")) * (2.0 / n))


def counts_grad_w(h, w):
    n = h.size
    a = np.convolve(w, 1.0 - h, "full")
    b = np.convolve(w[::-1], h, "full")
    return a[n - 1:2 * n - 1] - b[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _smooth_parts(h):
    n = h.size
    f = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, "full") * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(f.max())
    e = np.exp(OBJ_BETA * (f - m))
    s = float(e.sum())
    return m, e, s


def _obj(x):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    m, _, s = _smooth_parts(h)
    err = h.sum() - OBJ_TARGET
    val = m + math.log(s) / OBJ_BETA + 0.5 * OBJ_LAM * err * err + OBJ_RIDGE * float(np.dot(h, h))
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1e18
    return val


def _jac(x):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    m, e, s = _smooth_parts(h)
    w = e / s
    g = counts_grad_w(h, w) * (2.0 / n)
    err = h.sum() - OBJ_TARGET
    g += OBJ_LAM * err + 2.0 * OBJ_RIDGE * h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        g = np.zeros(n)
    return g


def lbfgs_refine(start, beta, lam, maxiter=150):
    global OBJ_BETA, OBJ_LAM, OBJ_TARGET, OBJ_RIDGE
    start = project_feasible(start, start.size)
    OBJ_BETA = float(beta)
    OBJ_LAM = float(lam)
    OBJ_TARGET = 0.5 * start.size
    OBJ_RIDGE = 1e-10
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * start.size
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _obj, start, jac=_jac, method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds,
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": int(maxiter * 10),
                     "ftol": 1e-16, "pgtol": 1e-10}
        )
        return project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), start.size)
    except Exception:
        return start


def full_refine(h, stop, betas, rng=None):
    best = project_feasible(h, h.size)
    bestc = eval_c5(best)
    n = best.size
    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() > stop - 25:
            break
        h2 = lbfgs_refine(best, beta, 1e5, 150)
        c2 = eval_c5(h2)
        if c2 < bestc:
            best, bestc = h2, c2
    if rng is not None and time.time() < stop - 60:
        noise = rng.randn(n)
        noise = np.convolve(noise, np.ones(7) / 7.0, "same")
        for sc in (0.04, 0.015):
            if time.time() > stop - 20:
                break
            st = project_feasible(best + sc * noise, n)
            h2 = lbfgs_refine(st, 800.0, 1e5, 100)
            c2 = eval_c5(h2)
            if c2 < bestc:
                best, bestc = h2, c2
    return best, bestc


def try_candidate(h, cur_h, cur_c):
    if h is None:
        return cur_h, cur_c
    h = project_feasible(h, h.size)
    c = eval_c5(h)
    if c < cur_c - 1e-14:
        return h, c
    return cur_h, cur_c


def grid_r(n):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    return np.abs(x - 1.0)


def triangle_h(n):
    return project_feasible(grid_r(n), n)


def sin_h(n):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.cos(math.pi * (x - 1.0)))
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def power_h(n, p):
    r = grid_r(n)
    h = ((p + 1.0) / 2.0) * np.power(r, p)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def sym_h(h):
    n = h.size
    return project_feasible(0.5 * (h + h[::-1]), n)


def make_binary(h):
    n = h.size
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    k = n // 2
    b = np.zeros(n)
    if k:
        b[np.argpartition(h, n - k)[-k:]] = 1.0
    return b


def binary_local(b, stop, rng, cur_h, cur_c):
    n = b.size
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return cur_h, cur_c
    b = make_binary(b)
    if b is None:
        return cur_h, cur_c
    h = b.copy()
    curc = eval_c5(h)
    best_h, best_c = cur_h, cur_c
    if curc < best_c:
        best_h, best_c = h.copy(), curc
    temp = 0.008
    while time.time() < stop:
        ones = np.nonzero(h)[0]
        zeros = np.nonzero(1.0 - h)[0]
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break
        i = int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
        j = int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
        h2 = h.copy()
        h2[i], h2[j] = 0.0, 1.0
        c2 = eval_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            h, curc = h2, c2
            best_h, best_c = h.copy(), c2
        elif c2 <= curc + 0.012 and rng.random() < math.exp((curc - c2) / temp):
            h, curc = h2, c2
        temp = max(0.0015, temp * 0.999)
    return best_h, best_c


def pl_objective(vals):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
        return 1e18
    h = np.interp(PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, vals)
    h = project_feasible(h, PL_N)
    return eval_c5(h)


def profile_vals(h, n, m):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    r = np.abs(x - 1.0)
    order = np.argsort(r)
    rs = r[order]
    hs = h[order]
    uniq, inv, cnt = np.unique(rs, return_inverse=True, return_counts=True)
    sums = np.bincount(inv, weights=hs)
    avg = sums / cnt
    return np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m), uniq, avg)


def pl_search(hbest, stop, seed, rng):
    global PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, PL_N
    n = hbest.size
    m = 15
    PL_GRID_R = grid_r(n)
    PL_KNOTS = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    PL_N = n
    best = project_feasible(hbest, n)
    bestc = eval_c5(best)
    bounds = [(-0.5, 1.5)] * m
    starts = [
        profile_vals(best, n, m),
        np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m),
        profile_vals(sin_h(n), n, m),
        profile_vals(power_h(n, 1.5), n, m),
    ]
    if differential_evolution is not None and stop - time.time() > 60:
        try:
            res = differential_evolution(
                pl_objective, bounds, seed=seed, maxiter=30,
                popsize=12, tol=1e-8, polish=False
            )
            if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                h2 = project_feasible(np.interp(PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, res.x), n)
                c2 = eval_c5(h2)
                if c2 < bestc:
                    best, bestc = h2, c2
                if res.fun < bestc + 0.01:
                    starts.append(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float))
        except Exception:
            pass
    for x0 in starts:
        if time.time() > stop - 18:
            break
        try:
            x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).copy()
            res = minimize(pl_objective, x0, method="Nelder-Mead",
                           options={"maxiter": 220, "fatol": 1e-10, "xatol": 1e-8})
            h2 = project_feasible(np.interp(PL_GRID_R, PL_KNOTS, res.x), n)
            c2 = eval_c5(h2)
            if c2 < bestc:
                best, bestc = h2, c2
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best, bestc


def resample_eval(h, n1):
    n0 = h.size
    x0 = (np.arange(n0, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n0)
    x1 = (np.arange(n1, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n1)
    h1 = np.interp(x1, x0, h)
    h1 = project_feasible(h1, n1)
    return h1, eval_c5(h1)


def final_n_sweep(h, cur_c, stop):
    best_h, best_c = h, cur_c
    for n1 in range(32, 1025, 16):
        if time.time() > stop:
            break
        h1, c1 = resample_eval(best_h, n1)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h = triangle_h(400)

    n = h.size
    if n < 32 or n > 2000:
        n = 400
        h = triangle_h(n)

    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h)

    cands = (
        triangle_h(n), sin_h(n), sym_h(best_h),
        power_h(n, 0.5), power_h(n, 0.75),
        power_h(n, 1.5), power_h(n, 2.0)
    )
    for cand in cands:
        best_h, best_c = try_candidate(cand, best_h, best_c)

    best_h, best_c = full_refine(best_h, t0 + 0.30 * total, [16.0, 64.0, 256.0], rng)
    best_h, best_c = pl_search(best_h, t0 + 0.60 * total, int(seed) + 1, rng)
    best_h, best_c = full_refine(best_h, t0 + 0.85 * total, [256.0, 1024.0, 4096.0], rng)

    if best_h.size % 2 == 0 and time.time() < t0 + 0.93 * total:
        best_h, best_c = binary_local(best_h, t0 + 0.93 * total, rng, best_h, best_c)

    best_h, best_c = final_n_sweep(best_h, best_c, deadline - 2.0)

    if time.time() < deadline - 35.0 and best_h.size <= 1000:
        best_h, best_c = full_refine(best_h, deadline - 2.0, [4096.0], rng)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_h.size)
    return best_h, eval_c5(best_h), int(best_h.size)
```
